## Case Studies

- [Ensuring the Safety of Astronauts with the Crew Module Uprighting System (CMUS)](https://www.oceaneering.com/case-studies/ensuring-the-safety-of-astronauts-with-the-crew-module-uprighting-system-cmus/) - We developed the Crew Module Uprighting System (CMUS) for the Orion spacecraft in response to a request from a leading aerospace and defense contractor.

- [Linear Valve Override Tool (LVOT)](https://www.oceaneering.com/products/linear-valve-override-tool-lvot/) - Linear Valve Override Tool (LVOT) enables controlled hydraulic and mechanical override of subsea linear push valves during ROV operations.
